      var dots = document.querySelector(".dots");
      var interval = setInterval(function () {
        if (dots.textContent.length >= 3) {
          dots.textContent = "";
        } else {
          dots.textContent += ".";
        }
      }, 300);

      function stopSpinner() {
        if (interval) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          interval = null;
        }
      }

      function showError(message) {
        stopSpinner();
        if (typeof message === "string") {
          dots.textContent = " Error: " + message;
        } else {
          dots.textContent = " Error";
        }
        console.error(message);
      }

      
      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }

      if (!window.crypto || !crypto.subtle || typeof TextEncoder === "undefined") {
        showError("Browser does not support required crypto APIs.");
      } else {
        var difficulty = parseInt("16", 10);
        solve("26bccad0ef67e828", difficulty)
          .then(function (result) {
            return fetch("\/.netlify\/submit-challenge", {
              method: "POST",
              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
              credentials: "same-origin",

        }
      }

      function showError(message) {
        stopSpinner();
        if (typeof message === "string") {
          dots.textContent = " Error: " + message;
        } else {
          dots.textContent = " Error";
        }
        console.error(message);

      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }

      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }

        }
      }

      function showError(message) {
        stopSpinner();
        if (typeof message === "string") {

          dots.textContent = " Error";
        }

      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }

      var dots = document.querySelector(".dots");
      var interval = setInterval(function () {
        if (dots.textContent.length >= 3) {
          dots.textContent = "";
        } else {
          dots.textContent += ".";
        }
      }, 300);

      function stopSpinner() {
        if (interval) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          interval = null;
        }
      }

      function showError(message) {
        stopSpinner();
        if (typeof message === "string") {
          dots.textContent = " Error: " + message;
        } else {
          dots.textContent = " Error";
        }
        console.error(message);
      }

      
      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }

      if (!window.crypto || !crypto.subtle || typeof TextEncoder === "undefined") {
        showError("Browser does not support required crypto APIs.");
      } else {
        var difficulty = parseInt("16", 10);

      var dots = document.querySelector(".dots");
      var interval = setInterval(function () {
        if (dots.textContent.length >= 3) {
          dots.textContent = "";
        } else {
          dots.textContent += ".";
        }
      }, 300);

      function stopSpinner() {
        if (interval) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          interval = null;
        }
      }

      function showError(message) {
        stopSpinner();
        if (typeof message === "string") {
          dots.textContent = " Error: " + message;
        } else {
          dots.textContent = " Error";
        }
        console.error(message);
      }

      
      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }

      if (!window.crypto || !crypto.subtle || typeof TextEncoder === "undefined") {
        showError("Browser does not support required crypto APIs.");
      } else {
        var difficulty = parseInt("16", 10);
        solve("7eb6bd26a4eb0ab2", difficulty)
          .then(function (result) {
            return fetch("\/.netlify\/submit-challenge", {
              method: "POST",
              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
              credentials: "same-origin",
              body: JSON.stringify({
                token: "eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsImtpZCI6IlYrN1B4SXIvNmlaT1V0aS9QVmtGazBPYm9sQXJmdEhuVWltelVGd0UvVTgiLCJ0eXAiOiJKV1QifQ.eyJpc3MiOiJuZXRsaWZ5Iiwic3ViIjoiMTU3LjE0Mi4yNS44MiIsImF1ZCI6WyJzZWMtY2hhbGxlbmdlLm5ldGxpZnkuY29tIl0sImV4cCI6MTc4Mzk4NjEzNiwibmJmIjoxNzgzOTg2MDk2LCJpYXQiOjE3ODM5ODYxMDYsImNoIjoiN2ViNmJkMjZhNGViMGFiMiIsImRpZiI6MTYsInBvdyI6dHJ1ZX0.BzVFShuNR5yOOOqT_gfmBVBCpLd5dMbak2tPJ1zQxmM",

      var dots = document.querySelector(".dots");
      var interval = setInterval(function () {
        if (dots.textContent.length >= 3) {
          dots.textContent = "";
        } else {
          dots.textContent += ".";
        }
      }, 300);

      function stopSpinner() {
        if (interval) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          interval = null;
        }
      }

      function showError(message) {
        stopSpinner();
        if (typeof message === "string") {
          dots.textContent = " Error: " + message;
        } else {
          dots.textContent = " Error";
        }
        console.error(message);
      }

      
      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }

      if (!window.crypto || !crypto.subtle || typeof TextEncoder === "undefined") {
        showError("Browser does not support required crypto APIs.");
      } else {
        var difficulty = parseInt("16", 10);
        solve("6711f684cd0fd4fb", difficulty)
          .then(function (result) {
            return fetch("\/.netlify\/submit-challenge", {

      }, 300);

      function stopSpinner() {
        if (interval) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          interval = null;
        }
      }

      function showError(message) {
        stopSpinner();
        if (typeof message === "string") {
          dots.textContent = " Error: " + message;
        } else {
          dots.textContent = " Error";
        }
        console.error(message);
      }

      
      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }

      if (!window.crypto || !crypto.subtle || typeof TextEncoder === "undefined") {

      var dots = document.querySelector(".dots");
      var interval = setInterval(function () {
        if (dots.textContent.length >= 3) {
          dots.textContent = "";
        } else {
          dots.textContent += ".";
        }
      }, 300);

      function stopSpinner() {
        if (interval) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          interval = null;
        }
      }

      function showError(message) {
        stopSpinner();
        if (typeof message === "string") {
          dots.textContent = " Error: " + message;
        } else {
          dots.textContent = " Error";
        }
        console.error(message);
      }

      
      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }

      if (!window.crypto || !crypto.subtle || typeof TextEncoder === "undefined") {
        showError("Browser does not support required crypto APIs.");
      } else {

        solve("b978bf8d09fe388c", difficulty)

      var dots = document.querySelector(".dots");
      var interval = setInterval(function () {
        if (dots.textContent.length >= 3) {
          dots.textContent = "";
        } else {
          dots.textContent += ".";
        }
      }, 300);

      function stopSpinner() {
        if (interval) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          interval = null;
        }
      }

      function showError(message) {
        stopSpinner();
        if (typeof message === "string") {
          dots.textContent = " Error: " + message;
        } else {
          dots.textContent = " Error";
        }
        console.error(message);
      }

      
      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }

      if (!window.crypto || !crypto.subtle || typeof TextEncoder === "undefined") {
        showError("Browser does not support required crypto APIs.");
      } else {
        var difficulty = parseInt("16", 10);
        solve("06b82a9b57cac0bf", difficulty)

      var dots = document.querySelector(".dots");
      var interval = setInterval(function () {
        if (dots.textContent.length >= 3) {
          dots.textContent = "";
        } else {
          dots.textContent += ".";
        }
      }, 300);

      function stopSpinner() {
        if (interval) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          interval = null;
        }
      }

      function showError(message) {
        stopSpinner();
        if (typeof message === "string") {
          dots.textContent = " Error: " + message;
        } else {
          dots.textContent = " Error";
        }
        console.error(message);
      }

      
      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }

      if (!window.crypto || !crypto.subtle || typeof TextEncoder === "undefined") {
        showError("Browser does not support required crypto APIs.");
      } else {
        var difficulty = parseInt("16", 10);
        solve("05efeb93b1d1c8f5", difficulty)
          .then(function (result) {
            return fetch("\/.netlify\/submit-challenge", {
              method: "POST",
              headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
              credentials: "same-origin",
              body: JSON.stringify({

                nonce: result.nonce,
              }),

      var dots = document.querySelector(".dots");
      var interval = setInterval(function () {
        if (dots.textContent.length >= 3) {
          dots.textContent = "";
        } else {
          dots.textContent += ".";
        }
      }, 300);

      function stopSpinner() {
        if (interval) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          interval = null;
        }
      }

      function showError(message) {
        stopSpinner();
        if (typeof message === "string") {
          dots.textContent = " Error: " + message;
        } else {
          dots.textContent = " Error";
        }
        console.error(message);
      }

      
      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }

      if (!window.crypto || !crypto.subtle || typeof TextEncoder === "undefined") {
        showError("Browser does not support required crypto APIs.");
      } else {
        var difficulty = parseInt("16", 10);
        solve("6b2286da88f9dc12", difficulty)
          .then(function (result) {
            return fetch("\/.netlify\/submit-challenge", {

      var dots = document.querySelector(".dots");
      var interval = setInterval(function () {
        if (dots.textContent.length >= 3) {
          dots.textContent = "";
        } else {
          dots.textContent += ".";
        }
      }, 300);

      function stopSpinner() {
        if (interval) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          interval = null;
        }
      }

      function showError(message) {
        stopSpinner();
        if (typeof message === "string") {
          dots.textContent = " Error: " + message;
        } else {
          dots.textContent = " Error";
        }
        console.error(message);
      }

      
      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }

      if (!window.crypto || !crypto.subtle || typeof TextEncoder === "undefined") {
        showError("Browser does not support required crypto APIs.");
      } else {
        var difficulty = parseInt("16", 10);
        solve("14640969f140ad10", difficulty)
          .then(function (result) {
            return fetch("\/.netlify\/submit-challenge", {

      function stopSpinner() {

        }
      }

      function showError(message) {
        stopSpinner();
        if (typeof message === "string") {
          dots.textContent = " Error: " + message;

          dots.textContent = " Error";
        }

      function leadingZeroBits(num) {
        if (num === 0) return 32;
        var count = 0;
        for (var bit = 31; bit >= 0; bit--) {
          if ((num & (1 << bit)) === 0) {
            count += 1;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
        return count;
      }

      var encoder = new TextEncoder();

      async function solve(challenge, difficulty) {
        var target = Math.min(Math.max(difficulty | 0, 0), 32);
        var prefix = challenge + ":";
        var nonce = 0;
        var yieldInterval = 1000; 
        for (;;) {
          var data = encoder.encode(prefix + nonce);
          var digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
          var view = new DataView(digest);
          var firstWord = view.getUint32(0, false);
          if (leadingZeroBits(firstWord) >= target) {
            return { nonce: nonce, hash: firstWord };
          }
          nonce += 1;
          if (nonce % yieldInterval === 0) {
            await new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 0); });
          }
        }
      }
